```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,133 @@
+---
+name: maintainers-review
+description: Write and self-review Kueue changes against the review bar of Kueue maintainers, distilled from their real review comments. Use before pushing or opening a PR, when addressing review comments, or when asked to review a PR "like a maintainer".
+license: Apache-2.0
+metadata:
+  copyright: The Kubernetes Authors
+---
+
+# Skill: Maintainers Review
+
+The checklist below holds the requests that Kueue maintainers repeat most often in PR
+reviews. Apply it to your diff before you push, so the reviewer can spend their time on
+design. Each rule, with its reason and linked precedents, is in [rules.md](rules.md).
+
+## How to use
+
+- **Writing code or preparing a PR.** Walk the checklist for your diff. Fix `[simple]`
+  items directly. For the other rules, change the code or explain your choice in the PR
+  description.
+- **Reviewing a PR.** Report a violation with its rule ID (for example `MR-H1`) and the file
+  and line, only where the diff breaks the rule. Cite the rule and its precedent from
+  [rules.md](rules.md) in place of claiming that a named maintainer would object.
+- **Before pushing**, run `make lint-fix`, `make ci-lint`, `make lint-api` and `make verify`,
+  and regenerate generated files (`make generate manifests update-helm`) from their
+  sources. The checklist leaves out what these already check.
+
+## Where the rules come from
+
+They are distilled from the review history of eight current and emeritus Kueue
+approvers (2022 to 2026). Each profile holds more rules and precedents.
+
+| Reviewer | Standing | Profile |
+|---|---|---|
+| mimowo | approver | [reviewers/mimowo.md](reviewers/mimowo.md) |
+| tenzen-y | approver | [reviewers/tenzen-y.md](reviewers/tenzen-y.md) |
+| sohankunkerkar | approver | [reviewers/sohankunkerkar.md](reviewers/sohankunkerkar.md) |
+| mbobrovskyi | approver for releases and kueuectl, reviewer | [reviewers/mbobrovskyi.md](reviewers/mbobrovskyi.md) |
+| alculquicondor | emeritus approver | [reviewers/alculquicondor.md](reviewers/alculquicondor.md) |
+| ahg-g | emeritus approver | [reviewers/ahg-g.md](reviewers/ahg-g.md) |
+| gabesaba | emeritus approver | [reviewers/gabesaba.md](reviewers/gabesaba.md) |
+| kerthcet | emeritus approver | [reviewers/kerthcet.md](reviewers/kerthcet.md) |
+
+## Checklist
+
+### A. Diff and scope
+
+- [ ] `MR-A1` [simple] Change only the lines the fix needs; move code unchanged.
+- [ ] `MR-A2` Send side fixes and refactors as separate PRs; land prep PRs first.
+- [ ] `MR-A3` [simple] Add code with its first user; export only what other packages use.
+- [ ] `MR-A4` Take API changes and new mechanisms through a KEP first.
+
+### B. Simplicity and reuse
+
+- [ ] `MR-B1` [simple] Search for an existing helper, constant or pattern, and reuse it.
+- [ ] `MR-B2` [simple] Delete guards, variables and parameters that change nothing; derive state.
+- [ ] `MR-B3` [simple] Use early returns and named functions, not anonymous functions, for logic.
+- [ ] `MR-B4` [simple] Apply each change to sibling integrations and every similar place.
+- [ ] `MR-B5` [simple] Use named constants, `workload.Reference`, `sets.Set` and typed keys.
+- [ ] `MR-B6` Put feature code in its own file or type; keep generic code generic.
+- [ ] `MR-B7` Write the simpler code first; back performance claims with numbers.
+
+### C. Naming and Go style
+
+- [ ] `MR-C1` [simple] Give names that match the behaviour; rename when it changes.
+- [ ] `MR-C2` [simple] Follow local naming: getters without `Get`, setters without `Set`.
+- [ ] `MR-C3` Use existing Kueue terms and PascalCase kinds; spell names out for users.
+- [ ] `MR-C4` [simple] Use `len()`, `ptr.Deref`, `cmp.Or` and the established import aliases.
+
+### D. Comments and docs
+
+- [ ] `MR-D1` [simple] Write comments that explain why; delete ones that restate code.
+- [ ] `MR-D2` [simple] Update comments, `By` texts and docs that the change made stale.
+- [ ] `MR-D3` [simple] Mark temporary code with a tracking issue and its removal condition.
+- [ ] `MR-D4` Document new fields, enums and exported symbols with user-facing semantics.
+- [ ] `MR-D5` [simple] Update the site docs in the same PR; extend existing pages.
+- [ ] `MR-D6` [simple] Mirror mechanical fixes into zh-CN; leave new prose to its owners.
+
+### E. Logging, errors, events
+
+- [ ] `MR-E1` [simple] Pick log verbosity from the Logging Policy table.
+- [ ] `MR-E2` [simple] Use the propagated logger and `ctx`, with structured key/values.
+- [ ] `MR-E3` Return or log every error once; drop error returns that cannot fail.
+- [ ] `MR-E4` Panic only on internal contract breaks; validate user input.
+- [ ] `MR-E5` [simple] Emit events, conditions and metrics only on change; keep messages short.
+
+### F. Controllers and API calls
+
+- [ ] `MR-F1` Patch only on change, once per object, and select with indexes.
+- [ ] `MR-F2` Clean up in-memory state on every delete path; deep-copy shared objects.
+- [ ] `MR-F3` Handle `Create` after restarts and know the leader-election behaviour.
+- [ ] `MR-F4` Keep event handlers to filtering; retry by returning errors from `Reconcile`.
+- [ ] `MR-F5` Keep webhooks free of API calls, and check UPDATE as well as CREATE.
+
+### G. Feature gates, API, upgrades
+
+- [ ] `MR-G1` Gate new and risky behaviour; check the gate first; keep gate-off unchanged.
+- [ ] `MR-G2` [simple] Alpha off, Beta on; one versioned gate line named by scope.
+- [ ] `MR-G3` Add the smallest API a user story needs; reuse existing fields first.
+- [ ] `MR-G4` Follow Kubernetes API conventions: `Ref`, conditions, status, enum modes.
+- [ ] `MR-G5` Validate new fields strictly with markers or CEL, and test them.
+- [ ] `MR-G6` Keep old objects, configs and defaults working across upgrades.
+
+### H. Tests
+
+- [ ] `MR-H1` Show that each new test and branch fails without the change.
```

  <!-- coderabbit-ai-generated:v1 -->
  
  **<picture><img src="https://storage.googleapis.com/coderabbit_public_assets/review-comment-icons-v1/amber-16.svg" width="16" height="16" align="absmiddle" alt=""></picture> Minor** · **Maintainability & Code Quality** · **Quick win**
  
  **Exclude gate-off tests from the fail-without-change rule.**
  
  MR-H10 requires a gate-off case while a gate exists. A gate-off case that preserves unchanged behavior can pass both before and after the fix. MR-H1 requires every new test to fail without the change, so a reviewer following MR-H1 would flag that required test.
  
  Add an exception to MR-H1 in `rules.md` for tests that preserve unchanged behavior, such as gate-off cases. Make the matching checklist item at `SKILL.md:105` say the same thing. Keep the requirement for tests that cover changed behavior.

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,430 @@
+# sohankunkerkar: review profile
+
+In this data, sohankunkerkar comments now and then from 2025-03, becomes a regular
+reviewer from 2025-11, and first uses `/approve` on 2026-06-15 (PR #12279). Since then they
+approve often (113 `/approve`, 200 `/lgtm`). The review data has 729 threads (539 PRs, 190
+issues) and 2,766 of their own entries. 1,894 of those are on other people's threads: 896
+inline review comments, 770 conversation comments and 228 review bodies. The other 872 are
+replies on their own 176 threads. Their activity grows over time. On others' threads they
+wrote 54 entries in 2025-Q4, about 270 per quarter in 2026-Q1 and Q2, 549 in Q3 and 740 in
+Q4 (to 11-24). Where their inline comments land on others' PRs: KEPs 39% (350), test code
+17% (151), controllers/jobframework/job integrations about 13%, queue/scheduler caches
+about 10%, and scheduler, TAS, MultiKueue, webhooks, APIs and docs 1–3% each. DRA is the
+main subject. Most of their long threads are DRA KEPs and fixes (#13601, #14130, #10973,
+#9742, #10320, #18632). After that come elastic jobs/workload slices, WaitForPodsReady,
+partial preemption (#14589), TAS, custom metric labels and MultiKueue.
+
+## What they care about most
+
+Shares are rough: they come from keyword stats over about 1,500 substantive comments, and one
+comment can hit several themes. See Method.
+
+1. **Behavior traced through the code: edge cases, what really happens, races, and whether every stuck state has an exit and a message the user can act on** (the biggest share; about 22% of comments are questions, and most of those are "what happens when…").
+2. **KEP rigor and readability**: claims about today's code checked against main, scope, interactions, gates, validation, Beta/GA criteria, Alternatives, user stories that still hold, terminology, tables, each point said once, no implementation detail, known gaps written into Risks/Drawbacks, and the KEP kept in sync with the code (39% of their inline comments are on KEPs; prose and structure comments about 7% in 2026-H2).
+3. **Tests that actually prove the change**: missing cases (every new branch and each integration sharing the code), gate-off cases, testing through the real entry point with production-shaped inputs, and running the change with a mutation ("still passes without the fix") (17% of inline comments are on test files; test requests about 3%; mutation checks rise in H2).
+4. **Feature gates and upgrade or backport safety**: behavior with the gate on and off, crashloops on upgrade, cherry-pick shape (about 8% mention gates; about 3% upgrade or backport).
+5. **Release process hygiene**: release notes (they write 32 release-note blocks for authors), CP requests, rebases, `/kind`, PR title and description, `Fixes` vs `Part of` (CP/backport about 11% of entries in H2).
+6. **Simplicity and reuse**: drop dead or redundant code, reuse existing helpers, wrappers and patterns (about 7% "drop/remove", about 6% "reuse/already does").
+7. **Accurate names and comments**: names that match behavior and local patterns, comments that are not stale or wordy (about 6% naming, about 4% comment wording).
+8. **Log verbosity and performance on hot paths** (about 2–4% each; logging mostly in H1, before `logging_policy.md` fixed the levels in 2026-10).
+
+## Rules for authors
+
+Precedent format: `[PR #N](url) kind date [@ path:line]: "exact quote"`. Each quote is an
+exact, contiguous substring of the comment body in the review data.
+
+### Naming and style
+
+- **[simple] Make related names agree: constructor with type, option with its sibling option, function name with KEP term, new helper with the codebase's naming pattern.** They read mismatched names as bugs in the making. Why: about 8 comments in 7 PRs (#9232, #9323, #10156, #14130, #14154, #14589).
+  - [PR #14130](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14130#discussion_r4109128806) inline 2026-09-25 @ pkg/dra/claims.go:153: "nit: `chargeForFirstAvailable`, to match the KEP and the error messages?"
+  - [PR #10156](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10156#discussion_r3025568341) inline 2026-04-02 @ pkg/controller/core/workload_controller.go:137: "nit: `WithAdmissionFairSharingConfig` to be consistent with the localqueue_controller"
+  - [PR #9232](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9232#discussion_r2807029838) inline 2026-02-14 @ pkg/cache/queue/inadmissible_workloads.go:223: "The constructor says "Reconciler", type says "Requeuer". Pick one."
+  - [PR #14154](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14154#discussion_r3776095286) inline 2026-08-13 @ pkg/workload/workload.go:696: "nit: I'd change the naming to `subtractReplacedRequestsFrom` to match the codebase pattern"
+- **[simple] Name a function, helper or test case for what it actually does, and rename it when its behavior changes.** A stale name misleads the next reader. Test case names count too: say the resource, the outcome and the error kind the case really has. So do log, error and field-error messages: say exactly what the code did ("failed to persist … condition", not "cleared" when nothing is cleared), word a new message like its siblings in the file, and pass the dereferenced value, not a pointer, as `field.Invalid`'s bad value. `coding_guidelines.md` only asks for "human-readable messages". Why: about 9 comments in 9 PRs (#8963, #9894, #14562, #15983, #16217, #16253, #16723, #17526, #18632); messages add 4 comments in 3 PRs (#9808, #14562, #15888 twice).
+  - [PR #17526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17526#discussion_r4408227953) inline 2026-10-29 @ pkg/controller/jobframework/reconciler.go:1479: "This now writes the spec and removes the annotation, so the name is misleading. Maybe `SyncWaitForPodsReady`?"

+  - [PR #18632](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18632#discussion_r4577111319) inline 2026-11-15 @ pkg/dra/claims_test.go:1138: "nit: This case expects a rejection, so could the name say "is rejected as unmapped" instead of "is charged"?"
+  - [PR #15888](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15888#discussion_r4065434376) inline 2026-09-21 @ pkg/controller/core/workload_controller.go:207: "nit: nothing is cleared here as we set `Requeued=True` with `DRAResourcesResolved`. Maybe "failed to persist DRA resources resolved condition"."
+- **Don't give an existing Kueue term a new meaning, and don't coin new terms.** Overloaded words confuse users and readers. Why: about 5 comments in 4 PRs (#13601, #14227, #14589, #17500).
+  - [PR #14589](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14589#discussion_r4396895200) inline 2026-10-28 @ keps/975-partial-preemption/README.md:252: "Kueue already uses "reclaim" for `reclaimWithinCohort` (taking back borrowed quota)"
+  - [PR #14227](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14227#discussion_r4168326368) inline 2026-10-02 @ site/content/en/docs/tasks/manage/observability/configure_custom_metric_labels.md:82: ""Workload control metadata" is a new term, it isn't used anywhere else in the repo."
+- **[simple] Fix typos and drop stray or leftover lines before pushing.** They point these out with `s/x/y/g` or suggestion blocks. The `misspell` linter only catches common misspellings, so identifier and test-name typos still reach review. Why: about 8 comments in 7 PRs (#8579, #9232, #9808, #9894, #13601, #17804).
+  - [PR #9894](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9894#discussion_r2942710937) inline 2026-03-16 @ test/integration/singlecluster/tas/tas_test.go:590: "`s/limits-pnly/limits-only`"
+  - [PR #13601](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13601#discussion_r4109031609) inline 2026-09-25 @ keps/2941-DRA/README.md:2208: "stray line?"
+  - [PR #8579](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8579#discussion_r2839137471) inline 2026-02-23 @ pkg/controller/jobs/raycluster/raycluster_webhook.go:92: "Stray line, drop this change."
+  - [PR #9232](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9232#discussion_r2806963728) inline 2026-02-14 @ pkg/cache/queue/inadmissible_workloads.go:216: "`s/requeuering/requeuing/g`"
+- **Keep conditions and code readable: break down or name compound conditions (a small named helper), extract a self-contained block into a helper, and move a self-contained part of a very large file into its own file.** Why: about 6 comments in 6 PRs (#8341, #10320, #10785, #17147, #18127, #19172).
+  - [PR #19172](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/19172#discussion_r4647813214) inline 2026-11-20 @ pkg/dra/claims.go:658: "claims.go is past 1,000 lines now."
+  - [PR #18127](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18127#discussion_r4609150841) inline 2026-11-18 @ pkg/controller/core/workload_controller.go:1607: "could this four-line condition be a small named helper"
+  - [PR #17147](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17147#discussion_r4261957794) inline 2026-10-13 @ pkg/controller/jobframework/reconciler.go:594: "Could we break this condition down? It's hard to read with the negated compound at the end"
+
+### Comments and docs

+### Logging and events
+
+- **[simple] Don't log at V(0) for per-workload or per-cycle events. Follow the level table in `logging_policy.md`: per-object decisions and scheduler cycle progress at V(3), selected decision details at V(4), traces at V(5).** Default logs (`--zap-log-level=2`) must stay quiet. Why: about 8 comments in 5 PRs (#8579, #9808, #10156, #12419 four suggestions, #17649); all 2026-H1. (historical: their V(2) suggestions for per-workload scheduler logs in #12419 predate the logging policy of 2026-10-08, and #17227 then moved scheduler progress logs to V(3); use V(2) only for concise summaries of a material state change.)

+- **[simple] Log "should never happen" states (a failed revert, an invalid pool that means a driver bug) where default logs (`--zap-log-level=2`) show them, at the level `logging_policy.md` gives: V(1) for a rare anomaly confined to one controller or the scheduler, V(0) for an invariant violation affecting Kueue as a whole, `Error` only when it disrupts an operation.** Why: 3 comments in 3 PRs (#10366, #15612, #18133). (historical: they asked for V(2) on the invalid-pool log in #18133; the logging alignment #19034, approved by mimowo on 2026-11-19, moved that log to V(1), as the policy says.)

+- **[simple] Write status, Events and metrics only on a real change. A repeated reconcile, a repeated scheduling cycle or a restart must not re-emit an Event, count a metric again, re-patch an unchanged object or reset a condition's timestamp.** This fits `logging_policy.md` ("Prefer emitting an Event after a successful write"). It is not the dropped `Eventf`-format rule, which `govet` printf covers. Why: 4 comments in 4 threads (#11720, #14589, #16857, Issue #18371), plus issue analysis #8095.
+  - [PR #16857](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/16857#discussion_r4339639770) inline 2026-10-21 @ pkg/controller/core/workload_controller.go:262: "Could we emit only when the QuotaReserved reason or message changes"

+  - [Issue #18371](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/18371#issuecomment-6889752122) comment 2026-11-15: "Also, only patch when the assignment changes."
+- **Keep a metric's series in step with its object's lifecycle. Delete a series when its object or label goes away, report it again on recreate or resume, and update it on every return path (a requeue, a missing ClusterQueue, a stop policy), so no series is left stale or missing.** This is the opposite failure from the rule above. No contribution guide covers metric series, and it recurs in the custom-metric-labels Beta work they own. When the same gap affects a whole family of series, they accept one follow-up for all of them instead of a fix in one place (#18295). Why: about 6 comments in 5 PRs (#10185, #17413 twice, #18221, #19001, #19267), plus the issue audit #16840 and the deferral in #18295.

+  - [PR #19267](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/19267#discussion_r4661744205) inline 2026-11-23 @ pkg/controller/core/localqueue_controller.go:518: "With this check, nothing re-reports the AFS usage series on resume, so it stays missing until the next sample (up to `usageSamplingInterval`)."

+
+### Errors
+
+- **Don't drop errors silently: return them, report them in the field error, or log them as a diagnostic (V(3) at least), and keep "evaluated false" apart from "evaluation failed".** When you log one, follow `logging_policy.md`: no `V(n).Error(...)`, and log an operational error once at the higher control layer. Why: about 7 comments in 6 PRs (#9742 twice, #10320, #10973, #11832, #14131, #17649).
+  - [PR #10320](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10320#discussion_r3263580105) inline 2026-05-19 @ pkg/dra/counters.go:176: "Should we log the error here with V(3) instead of discarding it altogether?"
+  - [PR #14131](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14131#discussion_r3753581465) inline 2026-08-10 @ pkg/controller/core/localqueue_controller.go:226: "I think `usageErr` gets swallowed when `cqInCache` is true."
+  - [PR #9742](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9742#discussion_r2921064734) inline 2026-03-11 @ pkg/dra/claims.go:326: "Errors from DeviceMatches are silently treated as `no match`."
+- **Make invariants hold by construction, not by care: no manual unlocks, no nil used as a hidden flag, no invariant that only a comment keeps.** Why: 3 comments in 3 PRs (#10785, #15961, #17916).
+  - [PR #17916](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17916#discussion_r4403424218) inline 2026-10-28 @ pkg/controller/core/workload_controller.go:184: "Passing a nil cache to mean "skip the device check" is easy to hit by accident"
+  - [PR #15961](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15961#discussion_r4097426294) inline 2026-09-24 @ pkg/controller/core/workload_controller.go:807: "move the queueing so the invariant holds by construction rather than by comment."
+  - [PR #10785](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10785#discussion_r3224064335) inline 2026-05-12 @ pkg/cache/queue/manager.go:448: "The manual unlock is error-prone if someone adds an early return later"
+
+### Simplicity and reuse
+
+- **[simple] Don't commit dead, unused or unreachable code, guards that can't fire, or checks the apiserver already does.** One exception: they asked to keep a defensive check where a future caller would otherwise silently undercount (#9808, "it's still worth adding as defensive code"). Why: about 14 comments in 12 PRs (#8579, #8963, #9232, #9742, #11832, #14130, #14721, #15503, #16857, #17526, #18127, #18706).
+  - [PR #14130](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14130#discussion_r4109126182) inline 2026-09-25 @ pkg/dra/claims.go:88: "nit: the apiserver already rejects this, fine to drop?"
+  - [PR #14721](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14721#discussion_r3834985164) inline 2026-08-22 @ pkg/controller/jobs/statefulset/statefulset_reconciler.go:194: "nit: This guard can't fire any more."
+  - [PR #8579](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8579#discussion_r2839110040) inline 2026-02-23 @ pkg/controller/jobs/job/ray_utils.go:56: "This is unused dead code. Don't check in dead code. Add it when it's needed"
+- **Search for an existing helper or pattern before writing one, and keep one implementation, not two.** Why: about 15 comments in 13 PRs (#9232, #9755, #11420, #11832, #11997, #12045, #14562, #15612, #16253, #17526, #17916, #18632).
+  - [PR #18632](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18632#discussion_r4571411927) inline 2026-11-14 @ pkg/config/validation.go:580: "nit: Could we share these name checks with `validateDeviceClassMappings` in a small helper, so the two lists can't drift?"
+  - [PR #15612](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15612#discussion_r4018350441) inline 2026-09-15 @ pkg/cache/scheduler/snapshot.go:84: "I'd keep one, not two."
+  - [PR #11997](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11997#discussion_r3376470398) inline 2026-06-08 @ pkg/workload/workload.go:1981: "Please use `workloadslicing.IsElasticWorkload` instead."
+  - [PR #11832](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11832#discussion_r3416156519) inline 2026-06-15 @ pkg/util/tlsconfig/tlsconfig.go:122: "This is redundant as `cliflag.TLSCurvePreferences` already handles empty input and returns nil."
+- **[simple] Don't add a wrapper, exported variant or generic parameter that has one caller. Call the underlying function, or fold the helper into its only caller.** The dead-code rule doesn't cover live but needless indirection. This is about helpers that only forward a call or carry an unused option; a small named helper that makes a condition readable is still welcome (see the readability rule). Why: 3 comments in 3 PRs (#8579, #17119, #17388), plus #14227, where moving the fix into the setters would "drop the exported helpers".
+  - [PR #17388](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17388#discussion_r4460461330) inline 2026-11-03 @ pkg/cache/queue/manager.go:854: "nit: this is the only non-test caller, in the same package, so could we call `cq.requeueIfNotPresent(log, info, true, reason, quotaReservedReason)` and drop `RequeueIfNotPresentImmediately`?"
+  - [PR #17119](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17119#discussion_r4294898887) inline 2026-10-16 @ pkg/controller/admissionchecks/provisioning/provisioning.go:57: "nit: after the trim `isTerminal` only has one caller. Could we fold it into `hasLiveBooking`?"
+  - [PR #8579](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8579#discussion_r2839130989) inline 2026-02-23 @ pkg/controller/jobs/job/ray_utils.go:36: "The `controllerOnly` bool parameter makes this generic but only `isRayRedisCleanupJob` uses it with `controllerOnly=true`."
+- **Put the fix in the one place that covers exactly the paths that need it: a setter every path goes through, or the call sites, rather than a shared function that other callers also use.** Why: about 5 comments in 5 PRs (#10009, #14227, #15961, #17526, #18127).
+  - [PR #14227](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14227#discussion_r4168212912) inline 2026-10-02 @ pkg/controller/jobframework/utils.go:280: "filtering once in those two setters would cover every path and drop the exported helpers and the pod_controller.go change."
+  - [PR #10009](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10009#discussion_r2961407481) inline 2026-03-19 @ pkg/controller/jobframework/defaults.go:51: "Might be better to handle this at the webhook call sites instead of the shared function."
+  - [PR #17526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17526#discussion_r4408223320) inline 2026-10-29 @ pkg/controller/jobframework/utils.go:267: "Could we keep the gate check here at the call site, as main does, and drop it from `ParseWaitForPodsReady`?"
+
+### API, configuration and feature gates
+
+- **Check both gate states. Gate-off must keep today's behavior, and the gate check belongs in the outer guard. Write it as an explicit `features.Enabled(...)` check even when the data it reads only exists with the gate on, placed where the sibling code or main already has it (inside a shared lookup when sibling lookups check there, at the call site when main does), so the gated path is easy to find.** This is one of their most common correctness findings. Why: about 14 inline comments on non-test code in 11 PRs (#9808, #10320, #11997, #14778, #15503, #16123, #17526, #17991), plus gate-off test requests; the explicit-check part is 3 comments in 3 PRs, all 2026-11 (#18078, #18127, #18632), and the placement part follows #17526 and #18632.
+  - [PR #11997](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11997#discussion_r3376451022) inline 2026-06-08 @ pkg/scheduler/flavorassigner/tas_flavorassigner.go:64: "This block now runs for elastic workloads even when `ElasticJobsViaWorkloadSlicesWithTAS` is off."
+  - [PR #9808](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9808#discussion_r2984396041) inline 2026-03-24 @ pkg/config/validation.go:554: "but this rejects the config and prevents Kueue from starting."
+  - [PR #18078](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18078#discussion_r4497349407) inline 2026-11-06 @ pkg/cache/scheduler/clusterqueue.go:572: "nit: could this also check `features.Enabled(features.CustomMetricLabels)`, like the rest of this file, so the gated path is easy to find?"
+- **New startup validation must not crashloop a config that loads today, since a validation error exits the manager. On release branches put the refusal behind an off-by-default gate; on main gate it, warn first, or call it out as ACTION REQUIRED.** Why: about 6 comments in 6 PRs (#9808, #13989, #14227, #14756, #18599, plus their own #19158).
+  - [PR #14756](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14756#issuecomment-5388555585) comment 2026-08-23: "Validation failure calls os.Exit, so the manager crashloops on a 0.18.z or 0.19.z upgrade."
+  - [PR #14227](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14227#issuecomment-5898120091) comment 2026-09-29: "the startup refusal in `labelKeysToCopy` breaks existing configs, so let's follow up with a warning or put it behind a gate."
+  - [PR #13989](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13989#discussion_r3787233398) inline 2026-08-14 @ pkg/config/validation.go:529: "Should this one sit behind `KueueDRAIntegration`?"
+- **Give each Alpha behavior its own gate as a kill switch, even when the feature is opt-in or rides on a Beta gate.** They don't ask for a gate when the change only makes the disabled state fail safe (#10964: "that should be the permanent ungated behavior"). Why: about 5 comments in 5 PRs (#8551, #9251, #13494, #14802, #17804).
+  - [PR #13494](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13494#discussion_r3884123911) inline 2026-08-28 @ keps/13243-multikueue-ray-worker-autoscaling/README.md:317: "Can we either add a dedicated gate or say explicitly why riding a Beta gate is fine here?"
+  - [PR #8551](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8551#discussion_r2839389567) inline 2026-02-23 @ keps/7990-preemption-cost/kep.yaml:1: "I think we need to add a feature gate here."
+  - [PR #9251](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9251#discussion_r2837162818) inline 2026-02-22 @ keps/8902-flavor-aware-drs/README.md:280: "I'd lean toward having a `FlavorAwareFairSharing` gate anyway, for consistency with how Kueue handles other Alpha features"
+- **Before you add new API surface (a new condition, strategy enum value or metric), check whether the existing lifecycle or mechanisms already cover it, since API is hard to remove.** Why: about 4 comments in 4 threads (#8309, #12233, #17433, #18717).
+  - [PR #12233](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/12233#issuecomment-4759995860) comment 2026-06-20: "adds API surface that's hard to remove"
+  - [PR #18717](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18717#issuecomment-6865368289) comment 2026-11-13: "A max-age alert gets noisy on a busy cluster, and `kueue_unadmitted_workloads` plus the pending resource metric already cover stuck workloads."
+  - [Issue #8309](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/issues/8309#issuecomment-3706270546) comment 2026-01-02: "Adding a new strategy enum to fix this feels like the wrong layer."
+- **Define validation for every new field and annotation: bounds, relations to sibling fields, and what 0 or empty does. When input that is invalid on its own is not rejected at the webhook, say where it is refused and what the user sees.** They test the edge values themselves, for example a 0 that gives a requeue loop with no end. This is not a hard "reject at the webhook" rule: in #13601 they asked why static request shapes were runtime inadmissibility rather than webhook rejections, then agreed to leave the webhook move for Beta (the webhook doesn't read ResourceClaimTemplates, and moving one path alone would create an asymmetry). Why: about 6 comments in 6 PRs (#7370 two suggestion blocks, #13601, #14589, #14700, #15503, #17500).
+  - [PR #14589](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14589#discussion_r4396906475) inline 2026-10-28 @ keps/975-partial-preemption/README.md:252: "What validation do we plan for the new floor?"
+  - [PR #14700](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14700#discussion_r4644541535) inline 2026-11-20 @ keps/5133-capacity-buffer-support/README.md:404: "With `backoffLimitCount` gone, 0 here gives an immediate requeue loop with no end. Minimum 1, and base ≤ max?"
+  - [PR #13601](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13601#discussion_r4047082244) inline 2026-09-18 @ keps/2941-DRA/README.md:1869: "so why are they runtime inadmissibility rather than webhook rejections?"
+- **[simple] Use the right validation mechanism. The Configuration API is not a CRD, so it takes no kubebuilder markers.** Why: 1 explicit statement (#16535); today's `apis/config/v1beta2` has no `kubebuilder:validation` markers, so it matches the code.
+  - [PR #16535](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/16535#discussion_r4231293664) inline 2026-10-09 @ keps/16029-pod-local-metrics/README.md:134: "The Configuration API doesn't use kubebuilder validation markers because it isn't a CRD, so this one does nothing."
+
+### Tests
+
+- **Make sure the test fails without the fix. Revert the change, or corrupt the expectation, and run it.** They do this themselves and report the result. Today's `writing_tests.md` asks the same ("For a bug fix, add a case that fails without the fix"). Why: about 22 comments in 21 PRs, almost all from 2026-08 on (#14551, #14585, #14704, #14836, #15001, #16123, #17119, #17294, #17388, #18133, #18595, #18632, #19026, #19172).
```

  Fixed in 196289d00. None of the cited threads ask to corrupt an expectation; they all revert the fix or delete a line. The rule now says "Revert the change or delete a new line, and run it", and for a gate-off test the line to delete is the new gate check.
  
  _Reply drafted with AI assistance (Claude Code)._

### Review by [@reruno](https://github.com/reruno) (commented) — 2026-11-25T11:42:38Z

- **cmd/experimental/skills/maintainers-review/rules.md:462** by [@reruno](https://github.com/reruno) — 2026-11-25T11:42:38Z
```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,771 @@
+# Maintainers review: rules
+
+The full rules behind the [checklist](SKILL.md#checklist). Each rule says what to do and
+why, lists the reviewers whose profile has it, and links one or two of their comments as
+precedents. The links point to the exact comment, and the quotes are copied from it.
+More precedents, and each reviewer's process and focus, are in `reviewers/<login>.md`.
+
+These rules add to the
+[Coding Guidelines](../../../../site/content/en/community/contribution_guidelines/coding_guidelines.md),
+the [Logging Policy](../../../../site/content/en/community/contribution_guidelines/logging_policy.md)
+and [Writing tests](../../../../site/content/en/community/contribution_guidelines/writing_tests.md).
+Where they disagree, the guidelines win.
+
+## A. Diff and scope
+
+### MR-A1 [simple] Change only the lines the fix needs
+
+Keep formatting, blank lines and names as they are unless the change needs them. A
+small diff is faster to review and cherry-picks to release branches with fewer conflicts.
+Move code unchanged, in its own PR.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, mbobrovskyi, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, ahg-g, gabesaba, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#4086](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/4086#discussion_r1939288583): "nit, revert empty line changes since this is to be cherry-picked, so to minimize changes of unnecessary conflicts."
+- mbobrovskyi on [#11002](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11002#discussion_r3206550951): "Please revert the unnecessary removal of empty lines."
+
+### MR-A2 One concern per PR
+
+Send side fixes, refactors and "while I was here" changes as separate PRs. Land
+mechanical prerequisites (new helpers, renames, moves) first, in a small preparatory PR
+with no behaviour change. Tide squash-merges PRs, so split work into PRs, not commits. A
+focused bug fix can be cherry-picked on its own.
+
+Raised by: all eight reviewers.
+
+- mimowo on [#16098](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/16098#discussion_r4093436872): "This looks like a good fix / performance optimization unrelated to the PR, could you send a bugfix for that?"
+- mbobrovskyi on [#18880](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18880#issuecomment-6920805612): "Could we split this into two PRs? PRs are squashed on merge, so the commit split is lost."
+
+### MR-A3 [simple] Add code with its first user, and keep the exported surface small
+
+Add API fields, constants, options and code paths in the PR that uses them, and delete
+the ones your change leaves without a caller. Unexport what only its own package uses.
+External integrations build against `jobframework`, so change its exported functions
+only when the change needs it.
+
+Raised by: all eight reviewers.
+
+- mimowo on [#12378](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/12378#discussion_r3450676234): "This looks unused for now. Let's drop the unused ones out, so that we don't forget to add tests for them once they become used."
+- mbobrovskyi on [#18411](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18411#discussion_r4491181649): "Since this is now called only from cmd/kueue/main.go, I'd drop the exported wrapper"
+
+### MR-A4 Agree on the design before the code
+
+Take API changes and new scheduling mechanisms through a KEP (or a KEP update) first,
+then implement what the KEP says. Update the KEP in the same release when the
+implementation departs from it.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor.
+
+- mimowo on [#10587](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10587#discussion_r3102044825): "The API changes should first go via KEP design phase."
+- tenzen-y on [#6541](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/6541#pullrequestreview-3119290372): "This seems to be a new scheduling mechanism. So, could you open KEP"
+
+## B. Simplicity and reuse
+
+### MR-B1 [simple] Reuse before you write
+
+Search `pkg/util`, the test wrappers, `test/util/behavioral` and the sibling
+integrations for a helper, constant or pattern, and call it. Keep one implementation of
+each piece of logic: duplicates drift apart.
+
+Raised by: all eight reviewers.
+
+- mimowo on [#9726](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9726#discussion_r2906914378): "Instead of that use our helper in pkg/util/client/client.go"
+- sohankunkerkar on [#18632](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18632#discussion_r4571411927): "nit: Could we share these name checks with `validateDeviceClassMappings` in a small helper, so the two lists can't drift?"
+
+### MR-B2 [simple] Every guard, variable and parameter earns its place
+
+Delete guards that cannot fire, single-use variables and wrappers, parameters that
+always get the same value, and empty implementations of optional interfaces. Rely on
+Kueue's own API validation for its invariants; read external APIs (KubeRay, TrainJob)
+defensively. Derive values from existing state rather than storing a second copy. Pass a
+new optional dependency as a functional option (`...Option`).
+
+Raised by: all eight reviewers.
+
+- mimowo on [#14316](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14316#discussion_r4085483924): "no need o implement an optimal interface with empty implementation."
+- mimowo on [#3848](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/3848#discussion_r1886337797): "It duplicates the information which is already present. It is hard to assess correctness with the duplicated information."
+
+### MR-B3 [simple] Keep control flow flat and named
+
+Use guard clauses and early `return`/`continue`, and test the positive case first. Move
+compound conditions and non-trivial blocks (loop bodies, lock sections) into small
+named functions in the same file, in place of anonymous functions (local closures)
+that hold logic. In tests, chain wrapper methods or `Clone()` in place of closures.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, gabesaba, alculquicondor.
+
+- mimowo on [#18397](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18397#discussion_r4490374279): "Can you wrap this in a named function to reflect better on the intention of the code?"
+- gabesaba on [#5177](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/5177#discussion_r2079657152): "prefer guard clause to reduce nesting/improve readability"
+- tenzen-y on [#15885](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15885#discussion_r4174829380): "Instead of an anonymous function, we should define a dedicated function in this file."
+
+### MR-B4 [simple] Fix every similar place, siblings included
+
+When you change one integration, controller or test, apply the same change to its
+siblings (the other Job integrations, MultiKueue adapters, controllers), or say in the
+PR why a sibling differs. When a reviewer flags one line, fix every occurrence of the
+pattern in the PR before you push.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g.
+
+- alculquicondor on [#2026](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/2026#discussion_r1578316269): "Apply the same recommendation for all the other job types."
+- tenzen-y on [#5215](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/5215#discussion_r2084731063): "I have the same comments in other places."
+
+### MR-B5 [simple] Named constants and typed keys
+
+Replace magic numbers and string literals with named constants, existing API constants
+(`corev1.ResourceCPU`) or the object's own field (`lq.Name`). Key workload state by
+`workload.Reference`, use `sets.Set[T]` for membership, and give other keys and reasons
+a dedicated type.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, mbobrovskyi, gabesaba, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#17680](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17680#discussion_r4413978981): "the benefit of using workload.Reference is strong typing, plus it immediately informs the reader workload is the key."
+- tenzen-y on [#11943](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11943#discussion_r3364001251): "Can you make the delay time a constant at the top of this file?"
+
+### MR-B6 Put code where it belongs
+
+Put feature-specific code in its own file (`tas_spreading.go`) or type with its own
+lock, in place of new loose fields on big structs. Keep generic code generic: put
+integration-specific behaviour behind an optional `JobWith…` interface or in the
+integration's package.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, sohankunkerkar, gabesaba, alculquicondor, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#14820](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14820#discussion_r4008132846): "Please move the helpers t `tas_spreading.go` all the helper functions, by analogy to tas_elastic_worklaods.go and tas_balanced_placement.go"
+- mimowo on [#15677](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15677#discussion_r4079828497): "We should avoid Ray-specific "if" in the generic reconciler. I think we may introduce an optional interface like JobWithCustomEquivalenceOptions, …"
+
+### MR-B7 Start simple, and back performance claims with numbers
+
+Choose the simpler code until a profile or perf test shows a bottleneck. In the
+scheduler, caches and reconcilers, keep per-workload work cheap: no full scans or
+allocations per call. Put before/after numbers in the PR for performance work.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, gabesaba, alculquicondor, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#11210](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11210#discussion_r3315468352): "I would start simple, and complicate code when we prove this is a bottlneck."
+- sohankunkerkar on [#12520](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/12520#discussion_r3494856808): "is O(active + inadmissible) with a heap.List() allocation on every call."
+
+## C. Naming and Go style
+
+### MR-C1 [simple] Names say exactly what the thing is or does
+
+A name claims neither a narrower nor a wider scope than the code has. Rename a function
+when your change alters what it does. Pick a specific word over `info`, `result` or
+`common`.
+
+Raised by: all eight reviewers.
+
+- mimowo on [#14316](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14316#discussion_r4099429245): "I think the name of the function is misleading, because it will return true also for regular ElasticJobs"
+- sohankunkerkar on [#17526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17526#discussion_r4408227953): "This now writes the spec and removes the annotation, so the name is misleading. Maybe `SyncWaitForPodsReady`?"
+
+### MR-C2 [simple] Follow the local naming conventions
+
+Name getters after their field, without `Get`, and use `find` or `resolve` for functions
+that compute or search. Write wrapper setters without `Set`. Leave the type and the
+surrounding context out of a name (`wls`, not `workloadList`). Give exported and
+long-lived names full words. Match the names of sibling functions.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#10917](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10917#discussion_r3181439093): "nit, let's use "find" prefixes, get suggests a simple get operation as if the value was preconputed."
+- tenzen-y on [#15000](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15000#discussion_r4077609453): "NITS: Go uses field names as getter functions."
+
+### MR-C3 Use Kueue's vocabulary
+
+Reuse the term Kueue already has for a concept (admitted and inadmissible for
+workloads, `AdmissionCheck`, `reclaim` for borrowed quota), one term per concept. Write
+Kueue kinds in PascalCase (`ClusterQueue`, `Workload`), and spell names out in
+user-facing text (`MultiKueue`, not MK).
+
+Raised by: tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, gabesaba, alculquicondor, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- tenzen-y on [#11720](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11720#discussion_r3404494109): "The API should be descriptive. The `Checks` is a common word. So, we should use Kueue Kueue-specific `AdmissionCheck` terminology here."
+- ahg-g on [#135](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/135#discussion_r832063357): "We sort of adopted the terms admitted/inaddmissible rather than scheduled/unschedulable."
+
+### MR-C4 [simple] Use the Go and Kubernetes idioms the codebase uses
+
+Treat nil and empty slices the same and test them with `len()`. Read optional pointers
+with `ptr.Deref(p, default)`, default with `cmp.Or`, and compare with
+`Quantity.Equal`. Reuse the import alias the codebase already has; add one only on a
+collision, and then alias the non-stdlib package.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba.
+
+- tenzen-y on [#13224](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13224#discussion_r3892484276): "nit: Using cmp.Or capability."
+- mbobrovskyi on [#9781](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9781#discussion_r2912901559): "We are trying to avoid std aliases."
+
+## D. Comments and docs
+
+### MR-D1 [simple] Comments explain why
+
+Add a comment where a reader would ask why: a deliberate skip, a kept-on-purpose check,
+a non-obvious order. Delete comments that restate the code, and keep fix history in the
+commit message.
+
+Raised by: all eight reviewers.
+
+- mimowo on [#9615](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9615#discussion_r2929823945): "nit, but this is self explanatory. I think comments are useful if they bring some readabilty, here the comment just eats one line"
+- tenzen-y on [#13571](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13571#discussion_r3792140261): "It would be better to avoid leaving comments for HOW."
+
+### MR-D2 [simple] Update what the change made stale
+
+Fix every comment, godoc, `ginkgo.By` text and user doc that your change makes false.
+Carry the "why" comments over when you move or rewrite code.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba, kerthcet.
+
+- mbobrovskyi on [#17575](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17575#discussion_r4527225797): "With this change, two comments that explain the default LocalQueue guard are no longer accurate:"
+- sohankunkerkar on [#15503](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15503#discussion_r4031633019): "Comment says "only set when strictly positive" but we set it whenever the pointer is non-nil."
+
+### MR-D3 [simple] Temporary code names its exit
+
+Mark workarounds, compatibility code and fallbacks with a to-do comment that links an
+open tracking issue (`(#N): <what to do>`) and says what must happen before the code
+can go: a release, a gate graduation, a minimum Kubernetes version.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g.
+
+- sohankunkerkar on [#17526](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17526#discussion_r4408219986): "When do we stop reading the Workload annotation? I'd add that to the KEP so this fallback has a removal plan."
+- alculquicondor on [#245](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/245#discussion_r866852357): "You have to explain what is there \"to do\"."
+
+### MR-D4 Document new API and exported symbols for their reader
+
+Give every new field, type, enum and exported function a doc comment with its semantics,
+default and special values (0, unset). List enum values as "Possible values are:". API
+godoc is user-facing: use YAML field names, describe behaviour, and name the feature gate
+of a gated field or annotation.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#10610](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/10610#discussion_r3153211083): "List the possible values, say "Possible values are: " and describe their semantics briefly."
+- tenzen-y on [#17559](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17559#discussion_r4492181368): "API documentation is user-facing. So, we should use non-implementation-specific terms."
+
+### MR-D5 [simple] Ship user docs with the behaviour
+
+When a PR adds a user-visible feature, gate, annotation or integration, update the site
+docs in the same PR: extend the page that covers the topic, add labels and annotations
+to `site/data/labels_and_annotations.yaml`, and link the canonical page (feature-gate
+setup) in place of repeating it. Keep samples in `site/static/examples/` and include them.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- mbobrovskyi on [#17575](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17575#issuecomment-6772351377): "Could we also add a short note to `site/content/en/docs/tasks/run/deployment.md`?"
+- tenzen-y on [#13308](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13308#discussion_r4105052844): "Instead of a dedicated page, can you just add the description to"
+
+### MR-D6 [simple] zh-CN: mirror mechanical fixes, leave new prose to its owners
+
+Write new or changed prose in `site/content/en` only; the zh-CN owners translate it in
+a follow-up. Mirror mechanical fixes (commands, links, tables, typos, what
+`make i18n-verify` needs) into the zh-CN counterpart.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, mbobrovskyi.
+
+- mimowo on [#8376](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8376#discussion_r2667964884): "I would suggest to drop the translation form this PR. Translation can be done in a follow up PR, and we have dedicated reviewers / approvers for that."
+- mbobrovskyi on [#5899](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/5899#discussion_r2192528144): "Shouldn't we also update the zh-CN version?"
+
+## E. Logging, errors, events
+
+### MR-E1 [simple] Pick log verbosity from the Logging Policy table
+
+Rare anomalies at V(0)–V(1), main per-object decisions at V(3), decision details at
+V(4), traces at V(5). Default logs (V(2)) stay quiet on large clusters. When a branch
+"should never happen", log it in place of a comment.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g.
+
+- mimowo on [#17675](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17675#discussion_r4634625218): "nit, use v3 accorting to our logging policy"
+- tenzen-y on [#9795](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9795#discussion_r2919823066): "the default logs (V2) should be minimized as much as possible."
+
+### MR-E2 [simple] Use the propagated logger and context
+
+Take the logger from `ctrl.LoggerFrom(ctx)`, the one the reconcile propagates, and log
+with structured key/values, `klog.KObj` and lowerCamelCase keys, without re-adding keys
+it already carries. Pass the caller's `ctx` down (`t.Context()` in tests) in place of
+`context.Background()`.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g.
+
+- tenzen-y on [#6325](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/6325#discussion_r2245709937): "We should use the propagated logger from reconciler since it has reconcile request objects."
+- mimowo on [#8964](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8964#discussion_r2820930836): "Don't use background context, propagate from the top. This is important for logging"
+
+### MR-E3 Handle every error exactly once
+
+Return the error up the stack, or log it and say why it is skipped. Log it once: wrap
+with `%w` and context in place of a second log line. Drop the `error` return from a
+function that cannot fail.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g.
+
+- mimowo on [#11141](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11141#discussion_r3267445590): "do not silence errors, at least log them"
+- gabesaba on [#8709](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8709#discussion_r2774172902): "and is it correct to swallow the error here?"
+
+### MR-E4 Panic only on a broken internal contract
+
+When a bad value can come only from a Kueue programming error at an internal call site,
+`panic` with a message that names the contract. Log anomalies from cluster state at
+V(0)–V(1), and validate user input and return an error.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, alculquicondor, gabesaba.
+
+- mimowo on [#8993](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8993#discussion_r2770698746): "I would rather panic here with the message. I looked at all the so-far invocations and I think in all cases here should be no nil passed. This will …"
+- alculquicondor on [#2027](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/2027#discussion_r1584947024): "Use `panic` for developer errors."
+
+### MR-E5 [simple] Events, conditions and metrics change only on a real change
+
+Emit an Event, update a condition or count a metric only when the reason or message
+changes, so a repeated reconcile or a restart stays silent. Keep user-visible messages
+short and actionable, and wrap user-influenced text with `api.TruncateEventMessage` or
+`api.TruncateConditionMessage`.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, ahg-g.
+
+- sohankunkerkar on [#16857](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/16857#discussion_r4339639770): "Could we emit only when the QuotaReserved reason or message changes"
+- alculquicondor on [#176](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/176#discussion_r843140349): "I think the rest of the string only make sense as part of the log, not as a user-visible message."
+
+## F. Controllers and API calls
+
+### MR-F1 Send fewer API requests
+
+Patch only when the computed object differs, write all changes to one part of an object
+in one request, and decide inside loops but patch once after them. Select objects with a
+field index or label selector in place of listing a namespace and filtering in Go. Pass
+down an object the caller already fetched.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, ahg-g.
+
+- mimowo on [#13015](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13015#discussion_r3577039133): "Seems like we may be producing unnecessary API requests. Shouldn't we guard sending the request by equivalence check?"
+- mbobrovskyi on [#9091](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9091#discussion_r2794184748): "There could be thousands of pods in this namespace. We need to add indexer for this."
+
+### MR-F2 In-memory state is cleaned up and never shared by mutation
+
+Clean caches, trackers and metric series on every delete or finish path, from one
+helper, with a test that fails on a leak. Deep-copy before you change an object from the
+informer cache or a shared snapshot.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, gabesaba, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#18219](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/18219#discussion_r4602944076): "This is in-memory state. Do we have some unit or integration tests to confirm there is no leak?"
+- alculquicondor on [#191](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/191#discussion_r846321066): "This is modifying the object from the informer Cache."
+
+### MR-F3 Survive restarts and leader changes
+
+After a restart every object arrives as `Create`, so handle `Create` like an update of
+existing state, and rebuild caches from status. Know whether a new controller runs only
+on the leader, and test restarts with `fwk.StopManager`/`fwk.StartManager`.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, alculquicondor.
+
+- mimowo on [#7312](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/7312#discussion_r2546320227): "We need to also add the code to Create, the reason is that this event is called on Kueue restart."
+- tenzen-y on [#6601](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/6601#discussion_r2305216917): "Can you support LeaderElection?"
+
+### MR-F4 Event handlers filter; Reconcile does the work and retries
+
+Keep `Create`/`Update`/`Delete` handlers to cheap checks and enqueueing. Do API calls
+and heavy work in `Reconcile`, and return transient errors so the workqueue backoff
+applies, in place of a fixed `RequeueAfter`.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, alculquicondor, gabesaba.
+
+- mimowo on [#5873](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/5873#discussion_r2345015783): "it is performing heavy operations (API requests) in event handlers, which are supposed to be very fast"
+- mimowo on [#11943](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/11943#discussion_r3371526145): "Unconditional constant using generic `draRequeueDelay` delay on errors is usually a code smell, because it eliminates the controller-runtime native …"
+
+### MR-F5 Webhooks stay fast and handle UPDATE
+
+Send no API requests from webhooks or from functions they call (`PodSets()`); put
+writes in the reconciler, where they are retried. Check defaulting and validation on
+UPDATE as well as CREATE (running objects, objects from before the upgrade, the default
+LocalQueue), and test it.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, mbobrovskyi.
+
+- mimowo on [#9726](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9726#discussion_r2920021916): "we cannot call the Patch from within PodSets(), because PodSets() is called from webhooks for validation and defaulting. Those need to be super fast, …"
+- mbobrovskyi on [#17575](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17575#issuecomment-6522745235): "Default() calls ApplyDefaultLocalQueue on UPDATE as well."
+
+## G. Feature gates, API, upgrades
+
+### MR-G1 Gate new and risky behaviour, and check the gate first
+
+Put new Alpha logic and disruptive fixes (including fixes that will be cherry-picked)
+behind a feature gate. Check the gate first, with an explicit `features.Enabled(...)`
+where sibling code checks it, so the gate-off path runs today's code unchanged and does
+no extra work. Gate the registration of a new controller, index or watch.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, mbobrovskyi, alculquicondor, kerthcet.
+
+- tenzen-y on [#17781](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17781#discussion_r4645895869): "To clarify, all new alpha logic should be guarded by a feature gate."
+- mimowo on [#5861](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/5861#discussion_r2184621304): "nit, First check feature gate to ensure any "new" functions are not called when disabled"
+
+### MR-G2 [simple] Standard gate stages and entries
+
+Alpha is off by default, Beta is on. A bug-fix gate is Beta on main and ships as Alpha,
+off, on release branches. In `pkg/features/kube_features.go`, add one versioned line
+with the next unreleased minor version, link the KEP or tracking issue, and name the gate
+after its real scope with the area prefix (`TAS…`, `MultiKueue…`).
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y.
+
+- mimowo on [#15049](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/15049#discussion_r3915662157): "I think this is a bugfix and for bugfixes we rather introduce FGs which are enabled by default. When CPking for risky changes we sometimes change to …"
+- tenzen-y on [#13797](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/13797#issuecomment-5355108806): "Those cherry picks should have "0.18, Alpha, disabled by default" and "0.19, Alpha, disabled by default" changes."
+
+### MR-G3 Add the smallest API that a user story needs
+
+A field cannot be removed without a new API version. Reuse an existing field, condition
+or lifecycle first, then prefer the lightest option (hard-coded behaviour behind the
+gate, an alpha annotation). Extend an existing metric with a label before adding one.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- tenzen-y on [#5981](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/5981#discussion_r2293140047): "The API has highly maintainance costs since we can NOT break that w/o API version change"
+- mimowo on [#14554](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/14554#discussion_r3966756816): "Do we have use cases for this? If the use cases are unclear for now let's drop."
+
+### MR-G4 Follow the Kubernetes API conventions
+
+Object references end in `Ref`, sibling suffixes match (`…State`), state goes into
+`status.conditions`, and Kueue-computed values go into `status`. Use an enum `mode` in
+place of a bool, mark every field `+optional` or `+required`, and prefix new Go type names
+with the parent kind.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, alculquicondor, gabesaba.
+
+- tenzen-y on [#7570](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/7570#discussion_r2543766166): "This is a typical object reference pattern which should have a `ref` suffix."
+- mimowo on [#8971](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8971#discussion_r2888547079): "maybe instead of `boolean` we could have something more generic like enum `mode` with options like `Disabled` or `TokenReview`, we could then …"
+
+### MR-G5 Validate new fields strictly, with markers, and test it
+
+Add bounds (`MinItems=1`, `Minimum=1`) and small caps from the first release: relaxing
+later is compatible, tightening is breaking (gate it, or mark ACTION REQUIRED). Use CRD
+markers and CEL first and webhooks for the rest; the Configuration API is not a CRD and
+takes no markers. Test markers and CEL in integration tests.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, gabesaba, ahg-g, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#17559](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/17559#discussion_r4387154358): "Probably `+kubebuilder:validation:Minimum=1`? Using =0 is no-op"
+- tenzen-y on [#19348](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/19348#discussion_r4667362613): "Can you implement CRD marker test? We can implement those in integration test."
+
+### MR-G6 Keep existing users working across upgrades
+
+Say what happens to objects created by the previous version. Keep cleanup code for old
+state for two releases, keep a replaced flag or field working for one release, and make
+a new default map to today's behaviour. New startup validation must load every config
+that loads today. Treat renames of user-facing resources as breaking, in a dedicated PR.
+
+Raised by: mimowo, tenzen-y, sohankunkerkar, alculquicondor, kerthcet.
+
+- mimowo on [#8530](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/8530#discussion_r2681240298): "we cannot remove this code in the PR, because upgrading Kueue will not work - nothing will remove the finalizers from pre-existing workloads. So we …"
+- tenzen-y on [#9370](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/kueue/pull/9370#issuecomment-3936515271): "The problem is that if we decide to enable this by default now, we can not change the default (enabled -> disabled) in the future version because …"
+
+## H. Tests
+
+### MR-H1 Prove that every new test and branch can fail
+
+Revert the fix, delete a new line, or corrupt an expectation, and check that a test goes
+red. Say in the PR which assert fails without the change. Cover every new non-error path,
```

  Fixed in 196289d00: MR-H1 now says "Revert the fix or delete a new line", and asks to name the assert that fails without the change.
